### M28 · `issues:queue --outcome` corrections never reach /issues or the SessionStart hook: the reader substitutes the row's Detail for a single-ID queue row

`scripts/issues-report.mjs:97-111; .claude/hooks/issues-surface.sh:48-60 and 80-84; scripts/ledger-inbox.mjs:36 (QUEUE_FIELDS)` · process · **CONFIRMED** · safe_cleanup=false · lane L19 (L19-F3)

- **Defect:** On 2026-08-13 (315199c16) the report and hook began replacing a single-ID queue row's 'Outcome, gate, verification, and stopping condition' cell with the cited row's Detail cell. On 2026-08-22 (93af96cf8, ledger #M6JNR8) the `queue` request type made that same cell editable (`--outcome` is in QUEUE_FIELDS) and the issues skill tells operators to use it for re-grades. Two applied queue requests already carried `outcome` (#231 on 2026-08-22, #057 on 2026-08-23). Whatever an operator writes there is applied to the ledger and then silently dropped by both readers; only the agent-safe-wins classifier still sees it.
- **Trigger:** Any `npm run issues:queue -- '#id' --outcome "..."` on a queue row citing exactly one ID, then `/issues` or a new session.
- **Impact:** The stop condition / gate text the operator corrected is invisible to every session that reads the queue; sessions act on the row Detail instead. The comments in both files still claim 'no inbox request type reaches it', so a future maintainer will not know the substitution now hides real edits.
- **Proof:** Executed: buildIssuesReport() on a ledger whose queue row says `OPERATOR-CORRECTED OUTCOME via issues:queue --outcome` and whose row Detail says `ROW DETAIL TEXT` returns recommended[0].outcome === "ROW DETAIL TEXT". Real data: the #231 queue cell begins `RE-SCOPED 2026-08-22 (was A1 …)` while `issues:report` returns `PHASE 5.2 CONFIRMED SATISFIED …` (the Detail cell).
- **Fix sketch:** Either render both (Detail as prose plus the queue cell as 'gate/stop condition') in issues-report.mjs and the hook, or remove `outcome` from QUEUE_FIELDS and the skill's `--outcome` example so the field cannot be edited into a void; update the two stale comments either way.
- **Skeptic (CONFIRMED):** Reproduced end-to-end: the queue action can edit the outcome cell, the skill tells operators to do so for re-grades, and both readers replace that cell with the row Detail for every single-ID queue row, so the correction is invisible to /issues and the SessionStart hook. The two code comments still assert the pre-#M6JNR8 state. #M6JNR8 is a resolved row and does not cover the reader side, so not a duplicate. Medium: an operator's stop-condition/gate correction is silently discarded from the only surface sessions read. Fix sketch (render both, or drop `outcome` from QUEUE_FIELDS) does not touch ledger invariants; removing the field would need the two already-applied queue requests left as-is (immutable).

### M29 · Live Web-Vitals workflow defaults to three routes that now 307-redirect, so a default dispatch can never produce a verdict

`.github/workflows/live-web-vitals.yml:42 (default routes), scripts/summarise-web-vitals.mjs:296-305, tests/summarise-web-vitals.test.ts:30, src/app/(search-app)/dsm/page.tsx:18-25` · process · **CONFIRMED** · safe_cleanup=false · lane L22 (L22-F1)

- **Defect:** The `routes` dispatch input defaults to `/,/therapy-compass,/documents/search,/dsm,/forms`. Since PR #2157 (2026-08-19) and #2308 (2026-08-23) `/dsm`, `/forms` and `/therapy-compass` are compatibility redirects to `/?mode=<id>` (site-map.md:36,43,66; dsm/page.tsx calls `redirect(...)`). `summarise-web-vitals.mjs` deliberately treats a report whose final URL differs from the requested URL as incomplete evidence (`measuredRequestedPage` → `incompleteEvidence` → step fails). lighthouse-budget.json `$routes` records exactly this failure for the LOCAL checker and removed the three routes there on 2026-08-23, but the live workflow (last touched 2026-08-09) and its test fixture were left on the old list.
- **Trigger:** Anyone dispatching `Live Web Vitals baseline` without overriding `routes` (the documented way to re-check mobile LCP/CLS/INP after payload work).
- **Impact:** 3 of 5 routes × 2 strategies × 3 samples = 18 of 30 cells are rejected as 'measured a different page'; the summarise step exits non-zero and no baseline verdict is recorded, so the workflow the two audits hang their `#017` decision rule on is silently unusable with defaults. The header comment (lines 3-30) also still instructs recording the verdict against `#017`, which was CLOSED 2026-07-31.
- **Proof:** Executed: `node -e import('scripts/summarise-web-vitals.mjs').measuredRequestedPage({requestedUrl:'https://psychiatry.tools/dsm', url:'https://psychiatry.tools/?mode=dsm'})` → `false`; same page → `true`. `git log -- src/app/(search-app)/dsm/page.tsx` → 715abc383 2026-08-19, 576ac7b51 2026-08-23; `git log -- .github/workflows/live-web-vitals.yml` → last 7dd554037 2026-08-09. lighthouse-budget.json:52 quotes the identical symptom for the local gate. Not executed: an actual dispatch (provider-backed).
- **Fix sketch:** Change the default to `/,/documents/search` (or the `<mode>/search` result routes named in lighthouse-budget.json `$routes`), update tests/summarise-web-vitals.test.ts DEFAULT_ROUTES and tests/live-web-vitals-inputs.test.ts:12,33 to match, and add a static test that every default route resolves to a real `page.tsx` that does not call `redirect(` (mirroring the `$routes` rationale). Replace the `#017` instruction in the header with the current owner row.
- **Skeptic (CONFIRMED):** Every link in the chain is quoted: three default routes are server redirects, Lighthouse records finalDisplayedUrl, the summariser deliberately rejects a redirected report, and the local budget checker already documented the identical symptom on 2026-08-23 while the live workflow default was last edited 2026-08-09. An actual dispatch could not be run (provider-backed) but the summariser's own code path makes the default-dispatch failure deterministic. Medium process: the workflow the audits cite as the mobile-vitals instrument cannot yield a verdict with defaults, and its header points at a closed row. Fix (change default + fixture + a static test that default routes have a non-redirecting page.tsx) is confined to the workflow and its tests; `check:github-actions` pin rules are unaffected by an input default.

### M30 · check:medication-interactions runs in no CI job and no test compares the index to the snapshot, so a snapshot-only merge can ship a stale interaction index silently

`.github/workflows/ci.yml:234-236 (only check:medication-lexicon-report) and :387-389 (only check:therapy-data-index); package.json:221 (the only occurrence of check:medication-interactions — it is absent from verify:cheap:internal at package.json:87); scripts/verify-pr-local.mjs:134 (local-only)` · test-coverage · **CONFIRMED** · safe_cleanup=false · lane L03c (L03c-F4)

> **Correction (Stage 5, red team):** Adding either gate to verify:cheap:internal fails check:gate-manifest unless CLAUDE.md:98 ('36 static/consistency gates') and .claude/skills/gates/SKILL.md ('check N of 39') are bumped in the same change (check-gate-manifest.mjs:96-109). Adding check:clinical-hazard-controls also makes verify:cheap red on every shallow clone and, on 2026-11-23, for everyone.

- **Defect:** The drug-interaction evaluator reads data/medication-interaction-index.json, which is derived from data/medications-snapshot.json plus the lexicon. The freshness gate exists (build-medication-interaction-index.ts --check) and passed locally in this audit (531 rows), but it is wired only into verify:pr-local. CI runs the lexicon-report and therapy-index drift gates under the same static_heavy condition and skips this one; tests/medication-interaction-lexicon-coverage.test.ts:70-76 only checks the index against itself (row total vs its own sourceRowCount), never against the snapshot.
- **Trigger:** Any PR that edits data/medications-snapshot.json (new drug, changed Key Interactions row) or the lexicon without re-running `npm run medications:interactions`, pushed from an environment that skipped verify:pr-local (the bare-PR route in AGENTS.md explicitly permits this).
- **Impact:** New or renamed drugs would be absent from `names`/`bySlug`: evaluateMedicationInteractions reports dataAvailable=false (safe) for the viewed drug, but changed row text would render stale via `row.note` on the reverse path (medication-interactions.ts:292), and rows added to an existing drug would not alert at all. The therapy catalogue got exactly this gate in CI after the identical failure mode (#333 comment at ci.yml:230-233); the medication index did not.
- **Proof:** grep of ci.yml for `medication-interactions` returns nothing; `grep -o check:medication-interactions package.json | wc -l` = 1; no test file imports both medications-snapshot and medication-interaction-index (grep). The 2026-09-02 gate-consolidation audit inventories the gate as "local only, offline" (row 382) without raising it. Not executed in CI — traced.
- **Fix sketch:** Add `- name: Medication interaction index drift / if: static_heavy_changed / run: npm run check:medication-interactions` beside the lexicon step in ci.yml, and add it to verify:cheap:internal so check:gate-manifest counts it.
- **Skeptic (CONFIRMED):** Wiring gap is exactly as claimed; the coverage test is a partial guard (catches deletions/reorders only), so a snapshot row whose severity token is edited (e.g. LOW→HIGH) merged via the bare-PR route would ship a stale index tone with CI green. Medium: requires a process lapse but the consequence is a wrong clinical tone. Fix sketch is safe but note: adding the gate to verify:cheap:internal requires updating the gate manifest that `check:gate-manifest` pins, and the CI step must use the same static_heavy condition as :235.

### M31 · The AccessibleTable 320px mockup journey was silently deleted by a merge one day after it landed; README still says the fixture route backs a test

`tests/ui-tools.spec.ts (journey absent); src/app/mockups/accessible-table-browser-fixture/page.tsx:1-27; mockups/README.md (Document search & viewer table, row `accessible-table-browser-fixture` — "Fixture backing a component test — keep")` · test-coverage · **CONFIRMED** · safe_cleanup=false · lane L11 (L11-F1)

- **Defect:** PR #2006 (54585e98d, 2026-08-16) added the route and a Playwright journey `low-confidence AccessibleTable keeps its full missing-value phrase readable at 320px @mockup` (+52 lines in tests/ui-tools.spec.ts). Merge commit ab7c5134f (PR #2025, 2026-08-17) removed it: `git diff ab7c5134f^1 ab7c5134f -- tests/ui-tools.spec.ts` shows 89 deletions including `-await page.goto("/mockups/accessible-table-browser-fixture"...)`. No non-merge commit ever removed it (`git log -m -S"accessible-table-browser-fixture" -- tests/ui-tools.spec.ts` lists only the add commit and five merges). Today `rg accessible-table-browser` finds no test file at all.
- **Trigger:** Any change to AccessibleTable's low-confidence / missing-value rendering at 320px.
- **Impact:** The 320px low-confidence clinical-table proof that closed outstanding-issues row #237 ("Completed with a real Chromium mockup journey at 320x700") no longer runs anywhere, so a regression that truncates 'Not recorded' or the low-confidence warning in a clinical table would not be caught. The README status tells a maintainer the route is protected by a test when it is not, which is exactly the shape the PR #2204 walk-back warns about (a route that looks dead is kept for a test that no longer exists — or vice versa).
- **Proof:** not executed — traced: `git diff ab7c5134f^1 ab7c5134f -- tests/ui-tools.spec.ts | rg accessible-table` → `-  await page.goto("/mockups/accessible-table-browser-fixture", { waitUntil: "domcontentloaded" });`; `rg -l accessible-table-browser tests` → no files; `git show ab7c5134f --first-parent --stat -- tests/ui-tools.spec.ts` → `1 file changed, 20 insertions(+), 89 deletions(-)`.
- **Fix sketch:** Restore the journey from 54585e98d into tests/ui-tools.spec.ts (or a dedicated ui-accessible-table-mockup.spec.ts added to mockupSpecPattern in playwright.config.ts), run it once on chromium-mockups, and record in the README row which spec backs the fixture. The #Y30AXB diff-integrity floor (2026-08-31) would now catch this shape of loss on a PR diff, but not on a merge commit — worth a note in docs/agents/test-deletion-guard.md.
- **Skeptic (CONFIRMED):** Line-by-line reproduced: the journey was added by a non-merge commit and exists in no test today, and the only deletion is a merge commit. No guard: docs/agents/test-deletion-guard.md has no 'merge commit' text and check:diff-integrity runs on PR diffs. Not a ledger duplicate — #237 is closed and states the proof exists. Severity stays Medium: the README's false 'backed by a test' claim concerns a clinical-caveat legibility proof, which is the safety-adjacent exception to the docs-drift=Low rule. Fix (restore journey into a mockup-pattern spec) touches nothing load-bearing; restoring +52 test lines does not lower any diff-integrity floor.

### M32 · tests/ui-tools-show-all.spec.ts has been collected by no Playwright project since it landed on 2026-08-16

`playwright.config.ts:25-27,47 (matchers); tests/ui-tools-show-all.spec.ts:17-39; tests/playwright-pr-shards.test.ts:25; playwright.config.ts:23` · test-coverage · **CONFIRMED** · safe_cleanup=false · lane L14 (L14-F1)

- **Defect:** The spec's basename `ui-tools-show-all` is not in the top-level `testMatch` alternation, nor in `productionSpecPattern`, `mockupSpecPattern` or `seededSpecPattern`. PR #2008 (0b95d063b) added the spec and the component change but never touched playwright.config.ts (git log on the config shows no commit from that PR). Both parity guards only check one direction — that every file the production matcher selects is in a shard group, and that named specs are collected — so a file the matcher never selects is invisible to them: `npx vitest run tests/playwright-project-isolation.test.ts tests/playwright-pr-shards.test.ts` passed 15/15 today with the orphan present.
- **Trigger:** Any `npm run test:e2e*`, `verify:ui`, the PR shards, or the release matrix: Playwright's file matcher excludes the spec before project selection, so it never runs; even `npx playwright test tests/ui-tools-show-all.spec.ts` would report 'No tests found'.
- **Impact:** The phone launcher journey (`/?mode=tools` -> 'Show all' link visible, 48 px tap target, keyboard Enter navigation to `/tools`, 'Open PsychSift Search' link href) has had zero browser evidence for 17 days while appearing to be tested. `tests/ui-tools.spec.ts:592` covers only the different `/tools?q=unknown&run=1` no-match route. This is the same class of silent gap the repo already fixed for the activation spec (#JZA0XK).
- **Proof:** node regex evaluation of the four config literals against every tests/*.spec.ts printed `-	-	-	-	tests/ui-tools-show-all.spec.ts` (only file besides the visual-config-owned ui-visual-baseline with no match). `scripts/browser-test-plan.mjs:163-178` would classify the file as `unroutable` and escalate, which is fail-closed for edits but does nothing for a never-selected file. Not executed in Playwright — traced.
- **Fix sketch:** Add `tools-show-all` to the `testMatch` and `productionSpecPattern` alternations (and the `productionSpecFilePattern` mirror in scripts/playwright-pr-shards.mjs), assign it to a shard in `prUiSpecProfiles`, then add a two-way parity assertion in tests/playwright-project-isolation.test.ts: every `tests/ui-*.spec.ts` and `answer-progress-ui-smoke.spec.ts` on disk must match `testMatch` and at least one project pattern (excluding the visual-config specs).
- **Skeptic (CONFIRMED):** Line reads as claimed and the offline regex proof reproduces the orphan. The only on-disk parity guard (project-isolation :138) checks the opposite direction (no leakage into the seeded project), so it cannot catch a never-selected file; the phone-scroll sibling guard at :220-232 is scoped to `ui-phone-scroll*`. Impact is a silently untested phone launcher journey for 17 days, not a clinical or data path — Medium test-coverage stands. Fix is additive (extend two alternations + shard assignment + a two-way parity test); it touches playwright config and `scripts/playwright-pr-shards.mjs`, whose existing tests (playwright-pr-shards.test.ts) will need the new file added to a shard group — not an AGENTS.md never-touch surface. Note the spec's basename also collides with the `ui-tools` mockup arm only if someone writes `tools(?:-show-all)?`; a dedicated alternative is safer.

### M33 · check:clinical-hazard-controls proves file/symbol existence, not that the named tests assert the control, and it never runs in CI

`scripts/check-clinical-hazard-controls.mjs:157-163 (symbol regex over controlPaths only), :140-154 (tests: path shape + existence only); docs/clinical-hazard-controls.json:20-88; package.json:165,182` · test-coverage · **CONFIRMED** · safe_cleanup=false · lane L21 (L21-F1) · corroborates docs/audit/gate-consolidation-audit-2026-09-02.md:381 ('local only (governance:release)') — new evidence: the gate's assertion depth, not just its placement

> **Correction (Stage 5, red team):** Adding either gate to verify:cheap:internal fails check:gate-manifest unless CLAUDE.md:98 ('36 static/consistency gates') and .claude/skills/gates/SKILL.md ('check N of 39') are bumped in the same change (check-gate-manifest.mjs:96-109). Adding check:clinical-hazard-controls also makes verify:cheap red on every shallow clone and, on 2026-11-23, for everyone.

- **Defect:** The register is described as 'the current machine-checked status authority' (docs/clinical-hazard-analysis.md:5) and each hazard lists `controlSymbols` plus `tests` as proof. The validator only (a) regex-matches each symbol somewhere in the controlPaths source and (b) checks each test path exists and matches `tests/*.test.ts`. It never checks that any listed test references the control symbol or the control path, so a test file can be emptied of the relevant case, or the symbol can be renamed and re-added as a comment, and the gate stays green. Today the manifest already links proofs that do not name the control: `deriveTrust` (H2, H5) appears in none of tests/source-governance.test.ts, answer-render-policy.test.ts, rag-score.test.ts, rag-retrieval-row-contract.test.ts; `collectSourceCandidates` (H4) in none of its three tests; `neutralizeIdentityField` (H6) not in tests/rag-injection.test.ts; H3 lists tests/ward-output.test.ts, which has zero references to `formatAnswerRenderCopyText` (the behaviour is in fact pinned indirectly via deriveAnswerRenderPolicy / buildRagSourceBlock, but the gate cannot tell). The gate is also outside `verify:cheap` and `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (only `governance:release`, which is provider-backed), so a 2026-11-23 `reviewExpiresAt` lapse or a control-symbol rename produces no PR signal.
- **Trigger:** Any PR that renames/removes one of the 13 control symbols or deletes the relevant `it(...)` case; or the calendar reaching 2026-11-23 (manifest.reviewExpiresAt) — none of these fail a PR check.
- **Impact:** The clinician-facing hazard register (H1–H6 'partial' with named proofs) can silently stop being true while `CLINICAL_HAZARD_CONTROLS_PASS` keeps printing; the ISO-14971-style status authority overstates what is machine-checked.
- **Proof:** not executed — traced. scripts/check-clinical-hazard-controls.mjs:157-163: `for (const symbol of hazard.controlSymbols) if (!new RegExp(\b${symbol}\b).test(controlSource)) errors.push(...)` where controlSource = concatenated controlPaths only; tests are validated at :140-146 by existsSync + `/^tests\/.+\.test\.(?:ts|tsx)$/` only. grep counts (command above) show 0 hits for deriveTrust / collectSourceCandidates / neutralizeIdentityField / formatAnswerRenderCopyText in the tests the manifest cites for them. package.json:182 is the only script invoking it; ci.yml has no match.
- **Fix sketch:** In validateClinicalHazardControls, for each hazard require that at least one listed test imports (or references) at least one controlPath module or controlSymbol (regex over the test source), and add `check:clinical-hazard-controls` to verify:cheap:internal / the CI static-pr docs scope (it is offline, ~1 s, needs fetch-depth 0 which CI already uses at ci.yml:95,214,454,519). Add a unit case to tests/clinical-hazard-controls.test.ts for a test file that names no control.
- **Caution:** Do not loosen the existing date/commit/role checks; the audit marks the gate load-bearing.
- **Skeptic (CONFIRMED):** Both halves verified: the validator cannot detect a listed test that no longer exercises the control, and nothing in CI runs it, so the 2026-11-23 expiry or a symbol rename produces no PR signal. Not a ledger duplicate (the audit table row is not an open issue and does not cover assertion depth). Medium: governance-register truthfulness, no direct clinical number. Fix sketch is compatible with the audit's load-bearing note; adding it to verify:cheap:internal requires a gate-manifest update and means the PR gate will go red on 2026-11-23 unless the review is renewed — by design, but the owner should be told.

### M34 · Clinical Ask has had no user entry point since PR #2360; the flag would enable a server route that nothing in the UI can call, while the privacy assessment says the composer routes into it

`src/components/clinical-dashboard/use-clinical-ask-runner.ts:21-27 (only invoker chain); src/components/clinical-dashboard/global-search-shell.tsx:1133-1143; src/components/clinical-dashboard/mode-clinical-ask-surface.tsx:38,57,69; docs/privacy-impact-assessment.md:506-507` · docs-drift · **CONFIRMED** · safe_cleanup=false · lane L12 (L12-F3)

- **Defect:** `runModeClinicalAsk` is called only from retry/continue controls (`global-search-shell.tsx:1134`, `mode-clinical-ask-surface.tsx:57,69`, `clinical-ask-workspace.tsx:62-63`) that render only when `clinicalAskWorkspaceVisible(session)` is true (`use-clinical-ask-shell-state.ts:22-32`), which requires a session that only `runModeClinicalAsk` itself creates. The composer submit path (`submitSearch`) never calls it; `tests/ui-clinical-ask.spec.ts:52` asserts the 'Get Smart answer' button has count 0 and that zero Clinical Ask requests are made. The composer-actions file was deleted in commit e6bafd4ca (#2360 'Remove Clinical Ask controls from search composers'). Yet `docs/privacy-impact-assessment.md:506-507` (last touched #2496) states 'When the server enables a governed mode, the shared composer can route a clearly question-like typed input into Smart Clinical Ask.'
- **Trigger:** Operator sets CLINICAL_ASK_ENABLED=true expecting the feature described in the PIA/readiness checklist.
- **Impact:** Setting the flag exposes the server route (F2) and the layout capability projection without any clinical benefit, and the governance/privacy documents describe a user journey that does not exist — a reviewer reading the PIA would assess a data flow the product cannot currently start. ~1,236 lines of client components, the 182-line `use-clinical-ask-speech.ts` (no importer in src/), and 22 test files (2,163 lines, 73 cases) maintain code that is unreachable in practice. Triage candidate per docs/agents/dead-code-deletion.md, not a delete list.
- **Proof:** not executed — traced. `grep -rn 'runModeClinicalAsk(' src/components` returns only global-search-shell.tsx:1134 (retry); `grep -rn use-clinical-ask-speech src` returns no importer; `git log --diff-filter=D -- src/components/clinical-dashboard/clinical-ask-composer-actions.tsx` -> e6bafd4ca.
- **Fix sketch:** Decide and record one of: (a) keep dormant — fix the PIA/handover wording to say 'no entry point exists; activation requires reinstating a composer action', and add a readiness-checklist line for it; (b) re-add an explicit governed 'Ask' action behind the flag; (c) run the dead-code protocol on the client layer + speech hook while keeping the server contracts and tests. Do not delete anything in this audit.
- **Skeptic (CONFIRMED):** The circular dependency (workspace visible only after a run; runs only from the workspace) is confirmed from code, and the PIA describes a composer flow that no longer exists. Docs drift in the privacy assessment keeps it at Medium under the re-grade rule (false claim concerns privacy), although the error is in the conservative direction — it describes a flow that cannot start. Fix_sketch correctly defers deletion to the dead-code protocol; nothing load-bearing.

### M35 · Review-agent scopes point at pre-move `src/lib/rag-*.ts` paths; the `src/lib/rag*.ts` glob does not cover the protected `src/lib/rag/**` tree

**REMEDIATED (2026-09-30 tip).** Claude `rag-retrieval-reviewer` / `clinical-governance-reviewer` now scope `` `src/lib/rag/**` ``; pinned by `tests/agent-scopes.test.ts`. Do not reopen from this historical body.

`.claude/agents/rag-retrieval-reviewer.md:3,18,20,22; .claude/agents/clinical-governance-reviewer.md:18-19,43` · docs-drift · **CONFIRMED** · safe_cleanup=true · lane L20 (L20-F4)

- **Defect:** The RAG module now lives in `src/lib/rag/` (36 files: `rag.ts`, `rag-answer-support.ts`, `rag-quote-verification.ts`, `rag-source-block.ts`, `rag-eval-cases.ts`, `rag-answer-*.ts`...). `rag-retrieval-reviewer` scopes `src/lib/rag*.ts`, `src/lib/rag-answer*.ts`, `src/lib/rag-eval-cases.ts` and its description says 'Use when editing src/lib/rag*.ts'; `clinical-governance-reviewer` scopes `src/lib/{...,rag-source-block}.ts` and `src/lib/{...,rag-quote-verification,rag-answer-support,...}.ts`. None of those match a file today, and a `src/lib/rag*.ts` glob does not descend into the directory. AGENTS.md 'RAG ranking protection' names `src/lib/rag/**` as the flagged surface, so the two subagents that are supposed to review it are told to look elsewhere.
- **Trigger:** A session invokes either subagent on a change under `src/lib/rag/**` (the most churned and regression-prone surface per the agent's own text) or relies on the agent description for auto-selection.
- **Impact:** The reviewer either reports the scope files as missing or silently reviews only `citations.ts`/`clinical-search.ts` etc., missing answer-support, quote-verification and source-block changes — the grounded-evidence checks (agent §1) that decide whether a clinical answer is safe to ship.
- **Proof:** Brace-expanded every backticked path in the agent files: `no match: src/lib/rag-quote-verification.ts`, `no match: src/lib/rag-answer-support.ts`, `no match: src/lib/rag-source-block.ts`, `MISSING: src/lib/rag-answer*.ts`, `MISSING: src/lib/rag-eval-cases.ts`; `ls src/lib/rag` lists all five under the new directory; `src/lib/rag.ts` does not exist at the top level. Traced, not executed.
- **Fix sketch:** Replace the stale entries with `src/lib/rag/**` (and keep `src/lib/citations.ts`, `clinical-search.ts` etc. which still exist); update the description line to `src/lib/rag/**`. Consider a small test that brace-expands agent scope globs against the tree, like the path check used here.
- **Skeptic (CONFIRMED):** Every backticked RAG path in both reviewer agents is stale after the move into src/lib/rag/, and a `src/lib/rag*.ts` glob does not descend into the directory. No test or gate pins agent scopes; no ledger row mentions the agents. Kept Medium (not the default Low for docs drift) because the false scope concerns the clinical-governance reviewer's coverage of answer-support/quote-verification/source-block — the grounded-evidence checks AGENTS.md treats as safety surface — even though these subagents are advisory, not gates, and have Glob/Grep to find the files anyway. Fix sketch (point at `src/lib/rag/**`) touches agent prose only; it does not alter any RAG ranking surface or gate.

## 8. The closed-off and gated sub-projects

The owner asked for these to be audited as carefully as the live product. Each section states what the thing is, how it is gated, what data it can touch, how healthy its tests and documents are, and one recommendation. Finding ids refer to §5–§7.

### 8.1 Ward Flow (`/mockups/ward-flow`, formerly Ward Management) — closed sandbox

**What it is.** A 13,364-line synthetic patient-flow prototype for WA metropolitan psychiatry bed placement: 17 routes, 58 component and model files under `src/components/ward-management/`, a pure reducer, a demo clock, hand-authored fixtures, 55 Vitest files and 4 Playwright specs carrying 49 `@mockup` journeys. Phases 1–3 are complete; Phase 4 onward was never built. Its feature branch (PR #2466, 299 commits) was closed unmerged on 2026-08-31; nothing from it is on `main`.

**How it is closed.** Three independent controls, all traced: the prefix is on `DEVELOPER_GATED_PATH_PREFIXES` (`src/lib/developer-area/headers.ts:26-31`), so the production blanket 404 for `/mockups/**` lets it through only to `DeveloperAreaGate` in `src/app/mockups/ward-flow/layout.tsx`, which requires a signed-in administrator; `src/proxy.ts:60` owns the retired `constellation → network` 307; the Tools catalogue has **no** `ward-management` entry (`docs/codebase-index.md:526-531` still says it does — corrected in §11); the only way in is the developer hub panel, which labels it "Synthetic prototype, not clinical decision support"; and all 49 browser journeys are `@mockup`, i.e. advisory-only in CI.

**Data it can touch.** None outside the browser. Both trees and their nine indirect imports contain no `fetch`, Supabase or OpenAI call; the only persistence is a `localStorage` sidebar-collapse flag; fixtures carry only `WF-` identifiers, sex, age cohort, legal status and role labels (verified by grep and pinned by `tests/ward-model.test.ts:117` and `tests/ward-flow-contracts.test.ts:183`). The legal-figure guard (`tests/ward-legal-figure-guard.test.ts`) forbids any Mental Health Act figure, duration or section number from rendering; it is unusually well reasoned. Its one deliberate carve-out — transport/transfer-order deadlines that render "Legal timing breached" — was raised by the finder and **refuted** by the skeptic as an owner-scoped decision of 2026-08-23 (§13, L09-F4); it remains a wording the owner may want to revisit, not a defect.

**Test and document health.** The unit suite is strong (45/45 in the lane's focused run; green in the full suite). Two real gaps: the bed-release state machine that ledger row `#9GPWT3` calls "most likely to be wrong" has no test for `blocked→confirmed`, `confirmed→blocked`, `blocked→released` or any of its three refusal branches (L09-F2); and nine landed plans still carry 441 unchecked task boxes, which the dead-code gate reads as "in-flight scaffolding" and therefore pins 140 of the sandbox's 189 exports indefinitely (L09-F1). The demo-clock Playwright flake (`#1BHXEF`) has a traceable cause: `clock.install` keeps ticking while the provider re-reads the minute on every render (L09-F3, duplicate of the open row). Three documents describe the pre-sandbox world — the codebase index (three false claims, L09-F5), `docs/ward-management-mode-map.md` (says the role design "has not been built yet" and lists 8 of 17 routes, L09-F6) and `docs/ward-flow-context.md`. Ledger row `#YTR84P` cites `tests/ui-ward-morning.spec.ts`; that file exists only on the closed branch, and the row already says so, so the row is accurate.

**Recommendation.** Keep it exactly as it is: a gated sandbox. Do not archive it to docs and do not delete anything. Tick or retire the nine plans so the dead-code gate stops pinning the tree; add the six reducer tests; refresh the three route documents; and pause the Playwright clock properly.

### 8.2 Care Plan (`/mockups/care-plan`) — developer-gated prototype

**What it is.** A synthetic, memory-only prototype for people with recurrent psychiatric emergency-department presentations: 21 routes, 43 component files, a 1,976-line reducer with 29 action types, three print outputs, every fixture `SYN-`prefixed. Stage B; later routes are specimens.

**How it is gated.** Sound by trace: exact-prefix match in `src/proxy.ts` (so `/mockups/care-plan-archive` is not let through), `DeveloperAreaGate` in the subtree layout, no `route.ts`, `error.tsx`, opengraph, parallel or intercepting route under the prefix, every dynamic page validates its parameter against a finite synthetic list, and `generateStaticParams` is inert because the parent layout calls `headers()`. `tests/care-plan-route-files.test.ts` and `tests/proxy.test.ts` pin all of this (43 focused tests re-run green).

**Data it can touch.** Nothing persisted: both providers are `useReducer`-only; a grep for `localStorage`, `sessionStorage`, `indexedDB`, cookies, `fetch`, `console` and Sentry across both trees returns zero hits, and `tests/care-plan-route-files.test.ts:406` fails the suite if any appears. The only real-world data is three WA crisis-line numbers deliberately kept real on patient-facing print, with a `verifiedOn` date and no re-verification cadence (L08-F5, PLAUSIBLE, Low). The deferred governance decision (no numeric identification threshold) is recorded consistently in the spec, glossary, types and ledgers and guarded by tests; no document claims it decided.

**Test and document health.** Strong unit and DOM coverage (7 files, about 13k lines, all 29 actions referenced). Browser proof is advisory-only. The weak point is documents and memory: the "start here" document points at a merged, deleted branch and says three tasks are unstarted (L08-F2); `docs/README.md` does not list the spec, glossary or the 32-file folder (L08-F6); the hub card and three code comments describe August (L08-F3); and about 65 deferred minors plus nine parked review findings live only in `docs/care-plan/sdd-ledger.md`, with **zero** rows in `docs/outstanding-issues.md` (L08-F1). This is the one sub-project with no presence in the repository's cross-session memory.

**Recommendation.** Keep it. File one umbrella `/issues` row pointing at the sdd-ledger backlog, banner the stale start-here document, fix the hub-card copy, and add an age check on the crisis-number `verifiedOn` date.

### 8.3 Caring Contacts — the live workspace and its own database

**What it is.** A sealed, synthetic suicide-prevention caring-contacts workspace: 10 pages under `src/app/caring-contacts/`, 14 API routes, a 2,500-line Postgres store plus an in-memory twin, and its own eight-migration schema under `caring-contacts/supabase/migrations/` (20 tables). It is deliberately separate from the Clinical KB project: `assertNotClinicalKbProject` refuses the pinned project ref and byte-identity with the main database URL, applied in both `store.ts:64` and `pool.ts:25`, and `pool.ts` is the only place a `pg.Pool` is constructed — `handler.ts`, `demo-seed.ts`, `session.ts`, `service-state-view.ts` and `run-db-tests.mjs` cannot reach one without it (probe answered).

**How it is gated.** In any real production process every page calls `notFound()` and every API returns a 404 refusal, because `isCaringContactsDemoEnabled()` (`session.ts:36-49`) requires both `PLAYWRIGHT_OFFLINE_MODE` and `NEXT_PUBLIC_DEMO_MODE` and `src/instrumentation.ts` refuses to start production with the former. So the live site cannot reach patient-shaped data at all. **But the Tools catalogue still advertises the workspace as "ready" with an Open button** — a signed-in clinician on psychiatry.tools presses Open on a suicide-prevention tool and gets a bare 404 (L07-F1 / L23-F1, Medium, CONFIRMED; the 19 August listing decision predates the 22–23 August production lock and the two were never reconciled).

**Data it can touch.** Row-level security here is real, not decorative: all 20 tables are `ENABLE` + `FORCE` with team-scoped policies, every statement runs after `set local role caring_contacts_app` inside one transaction wrapper (`postgres-repository.ts:458-476`), so even CI's superuser login cannot bypass policies; `audit_events` are frozen against UPDATE and DELETE by trigger and revoked privilege; the local database suite (213 tests, run in this audit on PostgreSQL 16) pins all of it. Two mutating tables, `pathway_versions` and `retention_state`, sit outside the transactional audit guard (L07-F6, Low). `service_stops.note` free text still has no retention path (`#JZ8B36`, duplicate). Dispatch windows use fixed +08:00 AWST calendar days, correct for Perth. **These guarantees are Postgres-only:** a production process without `CARING_CONTACTS_DATABASE_URL` falls back to the in-memory store (`store.ts:44-66`), which has no audit freeze and no retention clock, so if the production lock were ever opened without that variable set, patient-shaped data would live in process memory with none of the protections above (Stage 5).

**Test and document health.** 70+ offline test files plus the database project, which CI now runs as a **required** job (`caring-contacts-db`, `ci.yml:1135`, since 2026-08-26) — the two open P2 ledger rows `#B0530F` and `#27TWKM` claiming otherwise are stale and duplicate each other (L07-F2). The programme's map documents are a week behind `main` (L07-F7). The most serious document problem is governance: the **P1** row `#1S81R8` (three unmitigated hazards blocking any real-patient pilot) rests on `docs/caring-contacts/hazard-log.md` and a message-review pack that exist in no commit of the full history (5,489 commits reachable from `HEAD`, 6,889 across all fetched refs), and the handoff's recovery command names a commit that does not exist (L07-F3 / L19-F2, Medium, CONFIRMED). `CARING_CONTACTS_DATABASE_URL`, the one variable that switches the workspace from memory to Postgres, is documented nowhere operators look (L07-F8).

**Recommendation.** Reconcile the catalogue card with the production lock (hide it, or show it as "demonstration only" with no Open action); recover or re-write the hazard log and review pack and commit them; close the stale ledger rows not already queued (`done` requests for `#B0530F` and `#27TWKM` are already pending in the inbox from PR #2527 — do not file them twice); keep the workspace locked exactly as it is until enterprise sign-on exists.

### 8.4 Caring Contacts mockup (`/mockups/caring-contacts`) — developer-gated design twin

13 routes, a 13-action reducer, a frozen reference renderer for the live workspace. Same gating as Care Plan (traced sound). Zero exported names are shared with the live workspace and neither imports the other; the mockup is referenced as the frozen approved-identity source by the route builder documentation, two tests and the open Phase 2B row `#EWWJVX`, so retirement is a question for after Phase 2B, not now. One reducer action (`resume-plan`) has no test of any kind (L08-F4). Both prototypes import eleven production primitives from `src/components/ui/**`; a change there does not flip the advisory browser lane (`ci-change-scope.mjs:86-116` only matches mockup paths), but the DOM suites that render those primitives do run in the required unit job, so the gap is browser-only (print cascade, forced colours), not silent.

### 8.5 Developer hub (`/mockups/development`) — administrator-only, and the one gated surface that reads real data

**What it is.** Ten panels: five from two committed JSON snapshots, four read live (corpus health and document count through the service-role client, ingestion jobs and clinical quality through admin-gated APIs), plus a clinical-answer-failures cross-reference built from the ledger and the repository's eval case list. It is the only entry to Ward Flow, Care Plan and the Caring Contacts mockup.

**Access boundary, verified.** Every one of the 12 service-role queries in `src/lib/developer-area/*.ts` carries `.eq("owner_id", <verified user>)` and each is preceded by a fresh `isAdministratorUser` check; a non-administrator reaches only the access-denied screen; no raw clinical query text is surfaced anywhere in the hub; the committed snapshots hold no emails, secrets or patient data. Two real gaps: **one public env flag, `NEXT_PUBLIC_MOCKUPS_ENABLED=true`, switches the administrator gate off in production for all four gated subtrees** (`developer-area-gate.tsx:19-21`, L10-F1, Low after re-grade because it needs an operator to set it, but it is the single most consequential misconfiguration in the repository); and the gate lives only in a layout, so client-side navigations between hub pages do not re-run it and the proxy marks but does not enforce it (L10-F2). The hub's own comment says the document count is scoped by row-level security; it is scoped by an application filter on a service-role client, and RLS is a dead backstop repository-wide (`#J43Z6B`) (L10-F3). Four open ledger rows about the hub describe defects already fixed (L10-F4). The polling endpoint `/api/ingestion/jobs` is administrator-gated but, unlike its sibling admin routes, has no rate limit (L10-F9).

**Recommendation.** Make the gate ignore the public opt-in flag in production and assert the flag in production readiness first; then the three one-line comment corrections and the four ledger closures.

### 8.6 The mockup corpus (75 non-gated route directories) and design-sync

**What it is.** 75 design-study routes under `src/app/mockups/`, 74 component files (48,703 lines) under `src/components/`, a shared shell wrapper with 38 per-route chrome suppressions, a mockup-only Tailwind sheet, 42 public PNG comps, and 55 design-sync previews. `mockups/README.md` gives each a status (prototype app, chosen design, active study, redirect, superseded, parallel draft).

**How it is closed.** Production access is blocked **at request time** (`proxy.ts:214` → 404, `layout.tsx:27` → `notFound()`), not at build time: every mockup chunk, including the gated prototypes' synthetic patient-like fixtures, is built and shipped and retrievable under `/_next/static/chunks/<hash>.js` if the hash were known; chunk names are hashed and only referenced from RSC payloads that 404, so enumeration is impractical, but `docs/agents/wiring-and-bundle-budget.md` should state this as the actual boundary. No production file imports a mockup module (grep zero hits, ESLint `no-restricted-imports` boundary, two boundary tests). Mockup CSS is confined by the nested layout import plus `@source not`.

**Health.** 34 of 75 routes have a spec or unit test; 41 have none and are documented as drafts. The one route the README calls "a fixture backing a component test" lost its 320 px AccessibleTable journey to a merge commit on 2026-08-17 — no non-merge commit ever removed it, and `check:diff-integrity` runs on PR diffs, not merges (L11-F1, Medium; the offline DOM test that remains is a partial guard). Two "legacy compatibility redirects" documented under Production behaviour return 404 in production (L11-F2). The generated site map says `robots.txt` disallows `/mockups`; it allows everything (L11-F3 / L23-F3). The mockups bundle ceiling has been re-baselined five times in 17 days (+129%) without a recorded decision (L11-F4). `knip`'s `**/*mockup*` ignore hides 300 files / 76,269 lines (22.8% of `src` TypeScript) from the unused-export gate, and because it matches basenames only, 49 of the 351 reported unused exports are mockup-directory noise (L11-F5, L17-F5). Three top-level PNGs in `public/mockups` carry the retired "Clinical Guide" brand and are absent from the README (L11-F7). All 55 design-sync previews resolve. No public image shows patient data or internal hostnames.

**Recommendation.** Restore the AccessibleTable journey; decide the redirect policy once (proxy or docs); fix the generator string in `generate-site-map.ts` (it feeds both the site map and the repo-awareness snapshot); treat the knip and PNG lists as owner-triage lists, never a delete list (PR #2204 precedent).

### 8.7 Clinical Ask — dormant (`CLINICAL_ASK_ENABLED=false`)

**What it is.** A mode-aware governed answer workflow with a three-tier evidence ladder (catalogue → owner-scoped RAG → OpenAI web search restricted to an eight-domain authority registry), deterministic response governance, an SSE route and a client workspace; merged in #2293, disconnected from the composer in #2360, superseded by provider-free Smart search (#2459/#2482). About 1,800 library lines, 1,236 client lines, 22 test files (73 cases, all green).

**Is dormancy real?** Partly. No provider call is reachable while the flag is off, and external evidence never fetches URLs itself (it delegates to OpenAI `web_search` with `allowed_domains`, then re-validates every result: https only, no credentials/port/IP literal, redirect host must equal requested host, injection screen, extract and result caps, `store:false`) — so classic SSRF is absent. But the stream route validates, authenticates and consumes the durable rate limiter **before** the flag check and then answers HTTP 200 with an error frame rather than 404 (L12-F2); nothing in the UI can start a Clinical Ask session any more (the workspace is visible only after a run, and runs only from the workspace — L12-F3), while the privacy impact assessment still describes the composer routing into it; and `/api/speech/transcribe` — the voice input built for Clinical Ask — is a **live, anonymous-callable, OpenAI-backed endpoint with no caller, no master-flag check, and the browser `Permissions-Policy` still grants the microphone** (L04-F1 / L12-F4, Medium, CONFIRMED). The Differentials and Specifiers catalogue tiers substitute the whole catalogue when nothing matches, so synthesis would always run on unrelated evidence if the feature were switched on (L12-F1).

**Recommendation.** Keep it dormant but make dormancy real: 404 the stream and transcription routes on the master flag before any database call and set `microphone=()`; fix the catalogue fallback; correct the six stale document statements. Archiving would discard a mature governance layer the owner may still activate; removal would touch about 3,300 source lines, 22 test files, an applied migration and eight documents and must go through the dead-code protocol — neither is justified while the activation gates remain open.

### 8.8 Therapy Compass — live, 205 records awaiting clinician sign-off

The therapy side of the clinical data assets is in good order: every record route and result card states the unsigned state, the brief and sheet routes 404 rather than render unsupported records, the hashed public bundles are pinned by `check:therapy-data-index` in CI, and the sign-off tool refuses non-interactive or batch attestations. All 205 records remain `needs_review`; ledger row `#SBKXZ7` is accurate. One PLAUSIBLE Low: records reached through "Related therapies" cards and pathway step rows carry no per-record review badge (L03c-F8), and the compare screen substitutes the review status for the Evidence row when the evidence level is empty, conflating evidence grade with review state.

### 10.1 Completeness critic — coverage verdicts

One critic agent, run after all lanes were verified, judged each lane and each top-level directory as clean, under-read, or unowned, using the finders' own file counts and coverage gaps and its own spot-reads of the largest files no lane mentioned. "Under-read" is a statement about breadth, not about a known defect.

| L02 — RAG answer path                                                                                               | **under-read** | rag.ts (4,352), rag-extractive-answer.ts (4,042), clinical-search.ts (2,161), deep-memory.ts and ~20 sibling files were sampled or not opened; correctness rests on the provider-backed golden canary that was not run. My spot-read of answer-verification.ts (1,606 lines, only named in a reviewer-agent file list) found fail-closed numeric verification with six test files — no new defect, but the surface remains the least-read high-consequence tree. |

### 10.2 Cross-cutting themes the lanes could not see

- Same-day doc correction (commit 1884f2385, after the lanes ran) fixed numbers in prose but not the enumerations and generated strings beside them: docs/codebase-index.md:54 now says 16 app modes while lines 55-56 still list 15 (the `sources` mode, src/lib/app-modes.ts:460, is missing), src/lib/developer-area/hub-panels.ts:134 still says 'all 15 modes', docs/design-system/SPEC.md:78 and DECISIONS.md:122 still say 'fifteen modes', and the rewritten Ward Flow sentence at codebase-index.md:527-531 is now ungrammatical. This is L18's stated residual risk (prose counts are not tied to generated sources) realised within hours.
- Browser-side clinical persistence that survives account transitions is a family, not three isolated defects: patient physiology profile (L03a-F2/L03b-F1), favourites pins (L03b-F5), the answer thread (L02-F3) and now the Caring Contacts plan draft (patient name + mobile in sessionStorage, CRIT-F6). src/lib/supabase/client.tsx:264-268, 389-402 clears three stores by hand; nothing enumerates every clinical browser store, so each new feature re-decides the rule.
- Rate limiting is applied per route by hand and nothing gates it: 10 of 60 API routes call consumeApiRateLimit; in the administrator ingestion family only ingestion/quality does (ingestion/batches, ingestion/jobs, ingestion/jobs/[id]/retry and /api/jobs do not, CRIT-F7); account/favourites and account/preferences have none. check:owner-scope mechanically checks owner_id but no equivalent check exists for the rate-limit guard.
- Maintained-document hygiene has no gate: two privacy governance documents are double-encoded (BOM + mojibake, CRIT-F4), six root docs and one Claude skill still embed one developer's Windows home path (CRIT-F3), and docs:check-links checks path existence only, so neither class is caught; the fix commit removed only the two performance.md paths L22 happened to cite.
- Two tests keep the suite green by asserting a recorded defect (the critic named three; Stage 5 showed tests/privacy-ui.test.ts pins only the heading "Who can access your data", not the 'no shared corpus' sentence of L21-F2): tests/ui-smoke.spec.ts:1478 pins the retired 'Clinical Guide' brand (L13-F4/L23-F2), and tests/medication-patient-alerts.test.ts:153-160 pins the contraindication-only unassessed behaviour of open P2 #5PQRSW (CRIT-F5). The ledger rows name none of the pins, so a fix lands as a red test with no warning.
- Env flags named in verified findings are read consistently where they go through src/lib/env.ts (CLINICAL_ASK_ENABLED, NEXT_PUBLIC_MOCKUPS_ENABLED, RAG_QUERY_HASH_SECRET, RAG_TEXT_WEAK_OR_RELAXATION); the inconsistent ones bypass the schema — CARING_CONTACTS_DATABASE_URL (config.ts:35 process.env, absent from env.ts, .env.example and check-env-parity's list; L07-F8), INTERNAL_SERVICE_KEY (proxy-auth-crypto.ts:4; L01-F2) and PLAYWRIGHT_OFFLINE_MODE (instrumentation.ts:30, proxy.ts:282, session.ts:49, demo-seed.ts:394 each with their own combination rule). No new flag-inconsistency beyond those already recorded.
- Cross-runtime constants that could have drifted did not: embedding model text-embedding-3-small (env.ts:93, worker via env, indexing-v3-agent:167), retention windows 30/90 days + hourly cache purge (migration 20260901033250 vs privacy-page-content.tsx:158 vs PIA), Sentry scrub hooks (server/edge identical beforeSend/beforeSendTransaction/beforeSendLog, sendDefaultPii false), verify:cheap gate counts (CLAUDE.md:98 '36 static' matches check:gate-manifest '39 total / 36 static').
- Semgrep residue outside the SEM lane's three triaged families (unsafe-formatstring 9, javascript-confirm 8, unsafe-dynamic-method 5, prototype-pollution 1, dockerfile 5, useless-ternary 2) was reviewed here and is benign: console template literals, Node readline confirm() in operator scripts, bounded record lookups, a numeric array index, multi-stage FROM aliases, a placeholder build ARG, and one dead ternary in a mockup (privacy-page-directions-mockups.tsx:439) mirroring SEM-shell-docker-secrets-F1.

### 10.3 Spot-reads of the largest files no lane cited

- src/components/caring-contacts/workspace/plan-wizard/plan-wizard.tsx (2,598 lines) + plan-draft.ts — patient name and mobile are drafted into sessionStorage key caring-contacts:plan-draft; three documented clear paths (tab close, activation, discard) omit app sign-out → CRIT-F6. Otherwise careful: single storage API, tested directory scan, reserved fictional mobiles.
- scripts/generate-design-system-adoption.mjs (2,031 lines) — offline generator; exactly one of --write/--check required (line 1981-1983), writes only under docs/design-system/; no network, no provider. Clean.
- src/components/clinical-dashboard/document-search-results.tsx (1,743 lines) — no dangerouslySetInnerHTML, no storage, one justified no-img-element disable for signed private URLs. Clean.
- src/lib/answer-verification.ts (1,606 lines; only named in a reviewer-agent file list) — verifyAnswerNumbers fails closed when no cited chunk resolves (lines 1375-1383), claim-scoped verification in applyNumericVerification (1498-1545); covered by tests/answer-verification.test.ts and five other suites. Clean.
- scripts/check-rag-phase-receipts.mjs (1,455) and scripts/eval-quality.ts (1,443) — receipts checker is pure file/schema validation; eval-quality requires OpenAI/server env only in openai provider mode and runs under scripts/run-eval-safe.mjs (npm eval:quality). Clean.
- src/components/caring-contacts/workspace/patient-overview.tsx (1,198) and src/lib/caring-contacts/in-memory-repository.ts (1,468) + caring-contacts-server/store.ts:44-66 — production without CARING_CONTACTS_DATABASE_URL gets an empty in-memory store, demo seed gated on PLAYWRIGHT_OFFLINE_MODE + CARING_CONTACTS_DEMO_SEED=on. Clean.
- data/calculators/evidence.json (unowned) + src/components/calculators/calculator-evidence.ts + calculator-fixtures.ts:732-760 + search-detail.tsx:410,485 — every active calculator cites source:governance whose url is a GitHub blob link into this repository, rendered as a clickable Source on the clinician page → CRIT-F8. Quarantine statuses (sadpersons not_for_active_use, mdq/ybocs permission_review_required) are consistent with activeCalculatorIds.
- src/lib/service-ranker.ts (head + exports), src/sentry.edge.config.ts (whole), src/app/api/search/interaction/route.ts:44-84 (authenticated insert with owner_id) — clean.
- supabase/.branches/_current_branch — Supabase CLI local-state file tracked since 24ce23c02 → CRIT-F9.

The one-line corrections actually applied on this branch are listed in §11.1 with before/after text.

## 12. Never touch / not recommended — restated explicitly

Nothing below is a recommendation of this audit, whatever a finding's fix sketch might tempt a reader into.

- **The RAG ranking and evaluation surfaces** (`src/lib/rag/**`, `clinical-search`, `retrieval-selection`, `released-search-order`, `ranking-config`, the eval harness, aliases, golden fixture and retrieval RPCs). Several findings live in or beside these files (M-class items in `clinical-safety.ts`, `source-text-sanitizer.ts`, `ward-output.ts`, `rag-query-guard.ts`, `get_related_document_metadata`). Every fix there needs the `RAG impact:` PR line and, for any retrieval or ordering change, the provider-backed canary pair — the 2026-07-20 refuted-approaches record is why. This audit changed none of them and recommends none of them be changed without that process.
- **The ledgers and inbox.** `docs/outstanding-issues.md`, `docs/branch-review-ledger.md`, `docs/branch-review-records/**` and `docs/outstanding-issues-inbox/**` are never hand-edited. Where this audit says a row is stale, the action is `npm run issues:done` or `issues:update`, from the owner's triage, not an edit.
- **`check:gate-manifest`'s one-way invariant** (CI must never run less than the local static set). M-class findings that ask for a gate to be _added_ to `verify:cheap:internal` or to `verify:pr-local` are compatible with it; nothing here asks for a gate to be removed. Adding one is not free, though: `check:gate-manifest` parses the counts in `CLAUDE.md` ("36 static/consistency gates") and the gates skill ("check N of 39"), so the counts must move in the same change (Stage 5).
- **`check:diff-integrity` floors and `diff-integrity.json`.** Not to be raised to clear a diff.
- **Supabase migrations.** Two findings (M-class in §6, `set_document_corpus_access_mode` and `check:migration-history`) would be fixed by a migration or a script change. Merging a migration deploys to the live clinical database within seconds; those fixes go through an approved window with a validation guard migration, never as a side effect of this audit's PR.
- **Dead-looking code.** The knip lists (351 unused-export rows, 49 of them mockup noise), the six mockup exports in the calculators barrel, the three ward exports, the archived scripts still wired to npm commands, the dormant Clinical Ask tree and the Caring Contacts mockup are **triage candidates with evidence, not a delete list**. PR #2204 was walked back seven times for exactly this; `npm run check:dead-code-candidate -- --diff origin/main` is mandatory before any of them move, and 441 unchecked plan boxes currently pin most of the ward tree.
- **The five gate-parsed sections of `AGENTS.md`.** One finding (L18-F4, Low) shows the claim about them is itself inaccurate; that paragraph is not corrected in this PR because the safe change is to the gate's own documentation, which needs the gate author's decision.
- **Test files.** No test is skipped, deleted or quarantined by this audit. Two findings (L11-F1, L14-F1) are about tests that _stopped running_; the fix is to make them run.
- **The mockup corpus, Ward Flow sandbox, Care Plan prototype and Caring Contacts mockup.** Kept as they are. Retirement of the Caring Contacts mockup is a question for after Phase 2B.
- **Auto-merge, force pushes, pushes to `main`.** The PR for this audit is a draft with no auto-merge. Its own gate, stated for the record: the branch is **not** docs-only, because `data/repo-awareness-snapshot.json` is a route-payload input for `scripts/ci-change-scope.mjs`, so the diff classifies as heavy static, build, UI and coverage scope. The local gate is `npm run verify:pr-local` (20 steps, all offline: the docs and ledger checks, lint, typecheck, the full unit suite, a production build, the RAG fixture and medication checks), proven by its closing summary reading `failed: (none)` and `not reached: (none)`. While the PR is a draft, CI runs `static-pr`'s heavy steps, `PR Policy` and `PR mergeability` only; `safety`, `coverage`, `build`, `ui-critical(-fast)` and `caring-contacts-db` run when it is marked ready for review.
